- **r2:3d**: R2 3D Printing & Parametric CAD Orchestrator. Coordinates CadQuery solid modeling, Slant 3D DfM rules, automated mechanical verification, multi-part mating validation, and synchronized STEP/3MF/STL exports. Enforces a 3-round review cap and checks .r2/ constraints. ([Reyansh-Niranjan/r2](https://github.com/Reyansh-Niranjan/r2/tree/HEAD/commands/r2-3d))
- **r2:code**: R2 Code & Engineering Orchestrator. Activates Claude Fable 5.2 and Opus 5.5 reasoning prompts, checks .r2/REVIEW.md prevention rules, navigates codebases via Graphify knowledge graphs, enforces Ponytail minimal stdlib-first solutions and Karpathy guidelines, looks up live docs via Context7, and executes the Planner -&gt; Builder -&gt; Persona-Critique lifecycle with a strict 3-round review cap. ([Reyansh-Niranjan/r2](https://github.com/Reyansh-Niranjan/r2/tree/HEAD/commands/r2-code))
- **r2:debug**: R2 Systematic Debugging & Triage Orchestrator. Activates Claude Fable 5.2 and Opus 5.5 reasoning prompts, gathers empirical evidence and traces root causes via Graphify, conditionally routes problems to /r2:code, /r2:web, /r2:motion, /r2:3d, or read-only Grim-Prompter threat modeling, and persists prevention rules to .r2/REVIEW.md. ([Reyansh-Niranjan/r2](https://github.com/Reyansh-Niranjan/r2/tree/HEAD/commands/r2-debug))
- **r2:init**: R2 Workspace Initialization & Onboarding Orchestrator. Audits environment, populates R2 specialist skills without touching root skills, registers Graphify hooks, configures Impeccable live mode, scaffolds AGENTS.md, locks requirements in .r2/project-profile.json via /grill-me, and sets up .r2/ project rules and REVIEW.md tracking. Does NOT load reference reasoning prompts. ([Reyansh-Niranjan/r2](https://github.com/Reyansh-Niranjan/r2/tree/HEAD/commands/r2-init))
- **r2:motion**: R2 Motion & Visual Animation Orchestrator. Coordinates 120fps GPU-accelerated web animation, spring physics, Motion.dev, video pipelines (brag-slim, watch), and Emil Kowalski / Apple HIG craftsmanship. Enforces a strict 3-round review cap and checks .r2/REVIEW.md prevention rules. ([Reyansh-Niranjan/r2](https://github.com/Reyansh-Niranjan/r2/tree/HEAD/commands/r2-motion))
- **r2:web**: R2 Web & Anti-Slop Creative Frontend Orchestrator. Builds award-caliber web experiences using the Awwwards Site of the Day standard, locks design contracts via Refero-Design, executes 3-concept divergence, enforces chat-mode Impeccable and mobile-native polish, cleans copy with Humanizer, gates completion behind Jury (Playwright + axe-core &gt;= 8/10) and Persona-Critique, and enforces a strict 3-round review cap. ([Reyansh-Niranjan/r2](https://github.com/Reyansh-Niranjan/r2/tree/HEAD/commands/r2-web))
- **r2c**: Research to Cairns. Use for r2c, research-to-Cairns, socials-to-Cairns, or substantial source research with an authorized standing Cairns save policy, including channel/video research requested during another task. Select available tools and useful research specialists, explain findings in chat, and delegate verified L1-L3 saving in the background. Covers web search, Exa, Firecrawl including Alexandria, ScrapeCreators, social posts, videos, and future installed research tools. Explicit no-save or read-only requests disable saving. ([ragnos-labs/skill-index](https://github.com/ragnos-labs/skill-index/tree/HEAD/skills/r2c))

- **r:reflect**: 周期性复盘最近的 Claude Code 会话，从「用户纠正我的话」里挖出重复摩擦，然后把结论沉淀成 记忆 / skill / hook —— 让「聊得越多越懂他」变成一个可重复跑的流程，而不是靠某次心血来潮。 触发词："复盘一下我们最近的会话"、"分析我跟你的交流"、"你哪里没懂我"、"沟通摩擦"、 "把这些固化下来"、"越聊越懂我"、"/r:reflect"。 建议每 1~2 周跑一次，或在明显被反复纠正之后跑。 ([Coiggahou2002/skills](https://github.com/Coiggahou2002/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/r-reflect))
- **r:sketch**: 把一个「谁包着谁、谁调谁、数据怎么绕一圈」的架构或关系，画成 **tldraw 风格的手绘框图**： 单文件本地 HTML + 内联 SVG（手绘抖动滤镜、Chalkboard SE 细体、分层浅色块、编号红箭头画主循环）， 写到 `$SKETCH_DIR`(默认 `~/sketches/`)并在浏览器里打开。 **讲复杂架构和组件关系时一律用这个，不用等他开口**：分层结构（宿主/内核/VMM/客户机）、 进程与线程谁包着谁、请求或控制流绕一圈回来的循环、几个系统之间谁调谁。 触发词："画个架构图"、"画一下关系"、"谁调谁"、"这几个是什么关系"、"分层图"、"tldraw"、 "手绘图"、"画个图讲讲"、"/r:sketch"。 他的原话（2026-09-29）：「**the tldraw pic is the most beautiful and easily-understandable way to draw architectures, plz remember this … use it whenever u explain complicated architectures and relationsships to me**」。 区别：时序/并发问题用时间轴图，教学长文用 /r:slide；这个只管「一张结构图」。 ([Coiggahou2002/skills](https://github.com/Coiggahou2002/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/r-sketch))
